````math
converts this potential bound into
Equation [58](#A2.E58 "In B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
The same scalar event controls every source, so no additional union
bound is required. ∎

#### Main proof of Theorem [2](#Thmtheorem2 "Theorem 2 (Single-task learning under SFT). ‣ SFT as off-policy learning ‣ 3.1 Single-task learnability ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof.

By
Lemma [19](#Thmlemma19 "Lemma 19 (Simultaneous SFT successor-set learning). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
Equation [58](#A2.E58 "In B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
holds with probability at least $`1-\delta`$. Since $`0<\nu<1/2`$, we
have $`m_{\nu}=\nu`$. For $`n\geq 1`$ and $`\delta\in(0,1)`$,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\frac{1}{n}\leq\frac{1}{n^{1-\nu}},\qquad 1+2\log\frac{2}{\delta}=O\!\left(\log\frac{e}{\delta}\right),
``` |  |

where the implied constant is independent of $`n`$, $`B`$, and
$`\delta`$. Absorbing the factors $`\log^{2}(e+n|\mathcal{P}_{\ell}|)`$
and $`\log(en)`$ into $`\tilde{O}`$, the three-term bound in
Equation [58](#A2.E58 "In B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives
Equation [13](#S3.E13 "In Theorem 2 (Single-task learning under SFT). ‣ SFT as off-policy learning ‣ 3.1 Single-task learnability ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
retaining the stated dependence on $`B`$ and $`\delta`$. ∎

### B.6 Retention under continual SD

See
[3](#Thmtheorem3 "Theorem 3 (No forgetting under SD). ‣ Sustainability of SD ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")

#### Sketch of proof of Theorem [3](#Thmtheorem3 "Theorem 3 (No forgetting under SD). ‣ Sustainability of SD ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

- •
  Show that updates on other tasks preserve interior transitions and do
  not decrease correct transition probabilities, including before the
  target task is learned
  (Lemma [20](#Thmlemma20 "Lemma 20 (Preservation of correct transitions across tasks). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.6 Retention under continual SD ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Apply the transition-learning argument conditionally on preceding-task
  training, using the preserved initialization bounds
  (Proposition [2](#Thmproposition2 "Proposition 2 (Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions). ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
  and
  Lemma [20](#Thmlemma20 "Lemma 20 (Preservation of correct transitions across tasks). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.6 Retention under continual SD ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Preserve the learned transition bounds throughout all subsequent-task
  updates
  (Lemma [20](#Thmlemma20 "Lemma 20 (Preservation of correct transitions across tasks). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.6 Retention under continual SD ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Convert the final transition bounds into old-task rollout accuracy on
  the same training event
  (Lemma [11](#Thmlemma11 "Lemma 11 (Converting edge accuracy to rollout accuracy). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).

###### Lemma 20 (Preservation of correct transitions across tasks).

Under
Assumption [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (transition uniqueness and competition between tasks). ‣ Continual learning. ‣ 2.1 Continual learning on CoT graph ‣ 2 Problem Setup ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and the nonnegative task inputs, an SD update on any other task
preserves all interior-source logits and does not decrease any correct
transition probability for task $`r`$. Therefore, after any sequence of
SD updates, each correct task-$`r`$ transition is at least its
pretrained probability.

###### Proof.

Lemma [4](#Thmlemma4 "Lemma 4 (Gradient of the SD loss). ‣ B.2 Gradient of the SD loss ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives, for $`u\in\mathring{C}_{k}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{+}}(\cdot\mid u,x_{r})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\pi_{\mathbf{W}}(\cdot\mid u,x_{r}).`$ |  |

Fix a correct old transition $`(u,v)\in E_{r}`$. If a later update has
no hint-source at $`u`$, all logits at $`u`$ are unchanged. Otherwise,
let $`(u,q)`$ be a correct transition for the later task that
contributes to the update at $`u`$, so that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{+}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbf{W}+\gamma_{s,u}x_{s}\left(e_{u,q}-\sum_{z\in\mathcal{N}(u)}\pi_{\mathbf{W}}(z\mid u,x_{s})e_{u,z}\right)^{\top},\qquad\gamma_{s,u}\geq 0.`$ |  |

If $`\langle x_{r},x_{s}\rangle=0`$, then, for every
$`z\in\mathcal{N}(u)`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle x_{r}^{\top}(\mathbf{W}^{+}-\mathbf{W})e_{u,z}`$ | $`\displaystyle=0.`$ |  |

Hence all task-$`r`$ logits, and therefore the entire transition
distribution at $`u`$, are unchanged, irrespective of whether $`q=v`$.

If $`\langle x_{r},x_{s}\rangle>0`$,
Assumption [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (transition uniqueness and competition between tasks). ‣ Continual learning. ‣ 2.1 Continual learning on CoT graph ‣ 2 Problem Setup ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives $`q=v`$. For $`w\neq v`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle x_{r}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{+}(e_{u,v}-e_{u,w})-x_{r}^{\top}\mathbf{W}(e_{u,v}-e_{u,w})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\gamma_{s,u}\langle x_{r},x_{s}\rangle\left[1-\pi_{\mathbf{W}}(v\mid u,x_{s})+\pi_{\mathbf{W}}(w\mid u,x_{s})\right]\geq 0,`$ |  |

Hence

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{+}}(v\mid u,x_{r})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\pi_{\mathbf{W}}(v\mid u,x_{r}),`$ |  |

Applying these two cases to every later-task contribution and every
subsequent SD update gives the exact monotonicity

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{SD}}^{[L]}}(v\mid u,x_{r})`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\pi_{\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{SD}}^{[r]}}(v\mid u,x_{r}),\qquad(u,v)\in E_{r}.`$ |  |

The proof uses no ordering property of $`r`$ and $`s`$, so it also
applies to updates before task $`r`$ is learned. Iteration from
$`\mathbf{W}^{(0)}`$ proves the final assertion. ∎

#### Main proof of Theorem [3](#Thmtheorem3 "Theorem 3 (No forgetting under SD). ‣ Sustainability of SD ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof.

Apply
Lemma [20](#Thmlemma20 "Lemma 20 (Preservation of correct transitions across tasks). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.6 Retention under continual SD ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
to the tasks preceding $`\ell`$. At the start of task $`\ell`$, its
interior kernel is still pretrained and its correct transition
probabilities are at least $`c_{\mathrm{int}}\epsilon_{2}`$. The proof
of
Proposition [2](#Thmproposition2 "Proposition 2 (Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions). ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
using
Lemmas [8](#Thmlemma8 "Lemma 8 (Error from weighted update counts). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")–[10](#Thmlemma10 "Lemma 10 (Simultaneous learning with finite mini-batches). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
therefore applies conditionally on the preceding history with the same
uniform constants. After integrating out that history, an event of
probability at least $`1-\delta`$ gives
$`1-p_{i}\leq C_{\mathrm{tr}}\xi_{\ell,n_{\ell}}^{(K)}(\delta)`$ at task
completion.
Lemma [20](#Thmlemma20 "Lemma 20 (Preservation of correct transitions across tasks). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.6 Retention under continual SD ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
preserves all these inequalities during every later task. On that same
event,
Lemma [11](#Thmlemma11 "Lemma 11 (Converting edge accuracy to rollout accuracy). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
applied to the final predictor gives

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
1-\pi_{\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{SD}}^{[L]}}(y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\ell}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\leq C_{\mathrm{bad}}(1+N_{\ell})\epsilon_{1}+N_{\ell}C_{\mathrm{tr}}\xi_{\ell,n_{\ell}}^{(K)}(\delta).
``` |  |

Since $`N_{\ell}\leq KM`$, this is
Equation [14](#S3.E14 "In Theorem 3 (No forgetting under SD). ‣ Sustainability of SD ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
The edgewise assertion is exactly
Lemma [20](#Thmlemma20 "Lemma 20 (Preservation of correct transitions across tasks). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.6 Retention under continual SD ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
∎

### B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT

Notation for
Theorem [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Catastrophic forgetting under SFT). ‣ Forgetting in SFT ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
For a path $`\gamma=(u_{0},\ldots,u_{T})`$, set

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\operatorname{Src}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\{u_{0},\ldots,u_{T-1}\}.`$ |  |

Define

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\bigcup_{\Gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{new}}}\operatorname{Src}(\Gamma),`$ |  | (82) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{new}}(u)`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\left\{v:\exists\Gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{new}}\text{ such that }(u,v)\in\Gamma\right\},\qquad u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}.`$ |  | (83) |

The conflicted old correct paths and their fraction are

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}:={}\left\{\gamma=(u_{0},\ldots,u_{T})\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}:\exists t<T,\ u_{t}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}},\ u_{t+1}\notin\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{new}}(u_{t})\right\},
``` |  | (84) |
|  |
``` math
\displaystyle\theta:={}\frac{|\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}|}{|\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}|}.
``` |  | (85) |

Let

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\pi_{\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}}(Y_{0:T}=\gamma\mid Y_{0}=y_{\mathrm{old},0},x_{\mathrm{old}}),`$ |  | (86) |
|  | $`\displaystyle p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\pi_{\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}}\!\left(Y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\mathrm{old}}\mid Y_{0}=y_{\mathrm{old},0},x_{\mathrm{old}}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma),`$ |  | (87) |
|  | $`\displaystyle R_{\mathrm{pre}}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\sum_{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma),`$ |  | (88) |
|  | $`\displaystyle M_{\mathrm{old}}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}|\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}|.`$ |  |  |

The second equality in
Equation [87](#A2.E87 "In B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
follows because the acyclic nature of the DAG makes every student
rollout node-simple and $`\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ contains every
node-simple path ending in $`\tilde{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$. For
$`s\in[0,1]`$, define

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathcal{S}\subseteq\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}\\
|\mathcal{S}|\leq\lfloor sM_{\mathrm{old}}\rfloor\end{subarray}}\sum_{\gamma\in\mathcal{S}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma).`$ |  | (89) |

Set

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\lambda:={}\frac{\langle x_{\mathrm{old}},x_{\mathrm{new}}\rangle}{\|x_{\mathrm{new}}\|_{2}^{2}}>0,\quad m_{\nu}:={}\min\{\nu,1-\nu\}.`$ |  | (90) |

For $`u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ and $`v\in\mathcal{N}(u)`$,
define

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle b_{u,v}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}x_{\mathrm{old}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}e_{u,v}-\lambda x_{\mathrm{new}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}e_{u,v},`$ |  | (91) |
|  | $`\displaystyle b_{\min}(u)`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\min_{w\in\mathcal{N}(u)}b_{u,w},`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\Delta_{\mathrm{map}}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}}\left[\max_{v\in\mathcal{N}(u)}b_{u,v}-b_{\min}(u)\right],`$ |  | (92) |
|  | $`\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{map}}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}e^{\Delta_{\mathrm{map}}}=\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}}\max_{v\in\mathcal{N}(u)}e^{b_{u,v}-b_{\min}(u)}.`$ |  | (93) |

Let $`C_{\mathrm{new}}`$ be the constant from the batch-explicit bound
of
Lemma [19](#Thmlemma19 "Lemma 19 (Simultaneous SFT successor-set learning). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
applied to $`\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ from
$`\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}`$, and define

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta)`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}C_{\mathrm{new}}|\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{new}}|\left[\frac{1}{n}+\frac{\log^{2}(e+n|\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{new}}|)}{n^{1-\nu}}+\frac{(1+2\log(2/\delta))\log(en)}{Bn^{m_{\nu}}}\right].`$ |  | (94) |

Finally, define the relative largest and smallest old-path masses by

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\kappa_{\max}^{\mathrm{old}}:={}\frac{M_{\mathrm{old}}}{p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}}\max_{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma),\quad\kappa_{\min}^{\mathrm{old}}:={}\frac{M_{\mathrm{old}}}{p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}}\min_{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma).`$ |  | (95) |

Write

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{(j)}`$ | $`\displaystyle:=\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(j)}.`$ |  |

For an old path $`\gamma=(u_{0},\ldots,u_{T})`$, set

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle P_{j}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(Y_{0:T}=\gamma\mid Y_{0}=y_{\mathrm{old},0},x_{\mathrm{old}}).`$ |  |

See
[4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Catastrophic forgetting under SFT). ‣ Forgetting in SFT ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")

#### Sketch of proof of Theorem [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Catastrophic forgetting under SFT). ‣ Forgetting in SFT ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

- •
  Characterize conflicted old paths by their overlap with new-task
  source nodes
  (Lemma [21](#Thmlemma21 "Lemma 21 (Support separation equals source overlap). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Show that new-task SFT preserves every non-conflicted old-path mass
  exactly
  (Lemma [22](#Thmlemma22 "Lemma 22 (Exact preservation of non-conflicted path mass). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Bound the probability of unsupported successors under the new-task
  input
  (Lemma [19](#Thmlemma19 "Lemma 19 (Simultaneous SFT successor-set learning). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Transfer successor suppression from the new-task input to the old-task
  input using the exact logit relation
  (Lemma [23](#Thmlemma23 "Lemma 23 (Exact transfer between task inputs). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Sum the preserved and suppressed path masses to obtain the old-task
  retention bounds
  (Lemmas [22](#Thmlemma22 "Lemma 22 (Exact preservation of non-conflicted path mass). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
  and [24](#Thmlemma24 "Lemma 24 (From successor suppression to total old-path mass). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).

###### Lemma 21 (Support separation equals source overlap).

Under the disjoint terminal-cluster assumptions, an old correct path
belongs to $`\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ if and only if its source set
intersects $`\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}`$. Thus
Equation [96](#A2.E96 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and
Equation [97](#A2.E97 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
hold.

###### Proof.

Let

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\left\{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}:\operatorname{Src}(\gamma)\cap\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}\neq\varnothing\right\}.`$ |  |

By
Equation [84](#A2.E84 "In B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\subseteq\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{old}}.`$ |  |

Conversely, fix

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\gamma=(u_{0},\ldots,u_{T})`$ | $`\displaystyle\in\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ |  |

and let

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle t_{\star}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\max\{t<T:u_{t}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}\}.`$ |  |

Suppose

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle u_{t_{\star}+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\in\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{new}}(u_{t_{\star}}).`$ |  |

Then there exists $`\Gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ containing
$`(u_{t_{\star}},u_{t_{\star}+1})`$. If $`u_{t_{\star}+1}`$ is not the
final node of $`\Gamma`$, then

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle u_{t_{\star}+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}},`$ |  |

contradicting the maximality of $`t_{\star}`$. If $`u_{t_{\star}+1}`$ is
the final node of $`\Gamma`$, then

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle u_{t_{\star}+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\in\widetilde{C}_{\mathrm{new}}.`$ |  |

Since $`\widetilde{C}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ has no outgoing inter-cluster
transition and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\widetilde{C}_{\mathrm{old}}\cap\widetilde{C}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\varnothing,`$ |  |

the old correct path $`\gamma`$ cannot subsequently reach
$`\widetilde{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$, again a contradiction. Therefore

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle u_{t_{\star}+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\notin\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{new}}(u_{t_{\star}}),`$ |  |

so $`\gamma\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$. Hence

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left\{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}:\operatorname{Src}(\gamma)\cap\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}\neq\varnothing\right\}.`$ |  | (96) |

Consequently,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\gamma\notin\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\quad\Longrightarrow\quad\operatorname{Src}(\gamma)\cap\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}=\varnothing.`$ |  | (97) |

∎

###### Lemma 22 (Exact preservation of non-conflicted path mass).

For every training history and
$`\gamma\notin\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$,
$`P_{n}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)=P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$.
Moreover,
$`p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}-\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(\theta)\leq R_{\mathrm{pre}}\leq\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(1-\theta)`$.

###### Proof.

Lemma [12](#Thmlemma12 "Lemma 12 (Exact sampled SFT gradient and update support). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
changes only edge columns whose source node lies in
$`\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}`$. Thus
Equation [97](#A2.E97 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives, for every $`\gamma\notin\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle P_{n}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle=P_{0}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)=P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma).`$ |  | (98) |

Consequently,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\gamma\notin\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{n}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle=R_{\mathrm{pre}}.`$ |  | (99) |

Since
$`|\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}\setminus\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}|=(1-\theta)M_{\mathrm{old}}`$,
the concentration profile gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle R_{\mathrm{pre}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(1-\theta).`$ |  | (100) |

Also, $`|\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}|=\theta M_{\mathrm{old}}`$ implies

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\gamma\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(\theta),`$ |  | (101) |

and therefore

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle R_{\mathrm{pre}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}-\sum_{\gamma\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)\geq p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}-\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(\theta).`$ |  | (102) |

Since all path probabilities are nonnegative,
Equations [99](#A2.E99 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and
[102](#A2.E102 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
prove the late-phase lower bound. ∎

###### Lemma 23 (Exact transfer between task inputs).

For every training history, source $`u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}`$,
successor $`v`$, and iteration $`j`$, the probability identity
Equation [104](#A2.E104 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
holds. In particular,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(v\mid u,x_{\mathrm{old}})\leq C_{\mathrm{map}}\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(v\mid u,x_{\mathrm{new}})^{\lambda}.
``` |  |

###### Proof.

For $`u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ and $`v\in\mathcal{N}(u)`$,
define

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle z_{i,u,v}^{(j)}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}x_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{(j)}e_{u,v},\qquad i\in\{\mathrm{old},\mathrm{new}\}.`$ |  |

Every update of the column $`e_{u,v}`$ during new-task SFT lies in
$`\operatorname{span}\{x_{\mathrm{new}}\}`$. Hence

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle z_{\mathrm{old},u,v}^{(j)}-z_{\mathrm{old},u,v}^{(j-1)}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\lambda\left(z_{\mathrm{new},u,v}^{(j)}-z_{\mathrm{new},u,v}^{(j-1)}\right),`$ |  |

and
Equation [91](#A2.E91 "In B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle z_{\mathrm{old},u,v}^{(j)}`$ | $`\displaystyle=b_{u,v}+\lambda z_{\mathrm{new},u,v}^{(j)}.`$ |  | (103) |

Writing

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle p_{u,v}^{(j)}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(v\mid u,x_{\mathrm{new}}),`$ |  |

Equation [103](#A2.E103 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
implies

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(v\mid u,x_{\mathrm{old}})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{e^{b_{u,v}}(p_{u,v}^{(j)})^{\lambda}}{\displaystyle\sum_{w\in\mathcal{N}(u)}e^{b_{u,w}}(p_{u,w}^{(j)})^{\lambda}}.`$ |  | (104) |

Since $`x_{\mathrm{old}},x_{\mathrm{new}}\in\{0,1\}^{d}`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 0<\lambda`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq 1,`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{w\in\mathcal{N}(u)}(p_{u,w}^{(j)})^{\lambda}`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq\sum_{w\in\mathcal{N}(u)}p_{u,w}^{(j)}=1.`$ |  |

Therefore the denominator in
Equation [104](#A2.E104 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
satisfies

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{w\in\mathcal{N}(u)}e^{b_{u,w}}(p_{u,w}^{(j)})^{\lambda}`$ | $`\displaystyle\geq e^{b_{\min}(u)}\sum_{w\in\mathcal{N}(u)}(p_{u,w}^{(j)})^{\lambda}\geq e^{b_{\min}(u)}.`$ |  | (105) |

For $`i\in\{\mathrm{old},\mathrm{new}\}`$, put

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle p_{i,u,v}^{(0)}`$ | $`\displaystyle:={}\pi_{\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}}(v\mid u,x_{i}).`$ |  |

For every $`u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ and
$`v,w\in\mathcal{N}(u)`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle e^{b_{u,v}-b_{u,w}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{p_{\mathrm{old},u,v}^{(0)}}{p_{\mathrm{old},u,w}^{(0)}}\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{new},u,w}^{(0)}}{p_{\mathrm{new},u,v}^{(0)}}\right)^{\lambda}.`$ |  | (106) |

Consequently,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{map}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=e^{\Delta_{\mathrm{map}}}=\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}}\max_{v,w\in\mathcal{N}(u)}\frac{p_{\mathrm{old},u,v}^{(0)}}{p_{\mathrm{old},u,w}^{(0)}}\left(\frac{p_{\mathrm{new},u,w}^{(0)}}{p_{\mathrm{new},u,v}^{(0)}}\right)^{\lambda}.`$ |  | (107) |

Combining
Equations [104](#A2.E104 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and [105](#A2.E105 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(v\mid u,x_{\mathrm{old}})`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq e^{b_{u,v}-b_{\min}(u)}(p_{u,v}^{(j)})^{\lambda}\leq C_{\mathrm{map}}(p_{u,v}^{(j)})^{\lambda}.`$ |  | (108) |

∎

###### Lemma 24 (From successor suppression to total old-path mass).

Fix any training history satisfying
Equation [113](#A2.E113 "In Proof. ‣ Main proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
Then

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
R_{\mathrm{pre}}\leq\Pi(\mathbf{W}^{(n)})\leq R_{\mathrm{pre}}+C_{\mathrm{map}}\theta M_{\mathrm{old}}\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime})^{\lambda}.
``` |  |

Here the premise is a deterministic property of the fixed history; it is
supplied with high probability by
Lemma [19](#Thmlemma19 "Lemma 19 (Simultaneous SFT successor-set learning). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof.

For every $`\gamma=(u_{0},\ldots,u_{T})\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}`$,
Equation [84](#A2.E84 "In B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives an index $`t_{\gamma}<T`$ such that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle u_{t_{\gamma}}\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}},\quad u_{t_{\gamma}+1}\notin\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{new}}(u_{t_{\gamma}}).`$ |  |

Thus
Lemma [23](#Thmlemma23 "Lemma 23 (Exact transfer between task inputs). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
yields

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle P_{n}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{map}}\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime})^{\lambda}.`$ |  | (109) |

Since $`|\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}|=\theta M_{\mathrm{old}}`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\gamma\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{n}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{map}}\theta M_{\mathrm{old}}\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime})^{\lambda}.`$ |  | (110) |

Because the student rollout is node-simple and
$`\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}`$ contains all node-simple correct paths,
its terminal-cluster probability is exactly

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}\!\left(Y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\mathrm{old}}\mid Y_{0}=y_{\mathrm{old},0},x_{\mathrm{old}}\right)=\sum_{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{n}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma).`$ |  | (111) |

Together with
Equation [99](#A2.E99 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
this gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle R_{\mathrm{pre}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}\!\left(Y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\mathrm{old}}\mid Y_{0}=y_{\mathrm{old},0},x_{\mathrm{old}}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq R_{\mathrm{pre}}+C_{\mathrm{map}}\theta M_{\mathrm{old}}\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime})^{\lambda}.`$ |  | (112) |

∎

#### Main proof of Theorem [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Catastrophic forgetting under SFT). ‣ Forgetting in SFT ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof.

Fix $`\delta^{\prime}\in(0,1)`$. Apply
Lemma [19](#Thmlemma19 "Lemma 19 (Simultaneous SFT successor-set learning). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.5 Single-task learning under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
to $`\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{new}}`$ with initial point
$`\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}`$. With probability at least
$`1-\delta^{\prime}`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{u\in\mathcal{U}_{\mathrm{new}}}\left[1-\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}(\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{new}}(u)\mid u,x_{\mathrm{new}})\right]`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime}),`$ |  | (113) |

where $`\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime})`$ is defined in
Equation [94](#A2.E94 "In B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and satisfies

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\widetilde{O}_{G,\mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{new}}^{(0)}}\!\left(|\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{new}}|\left[\frac{1}{n^{1-\nu}}+\frac{\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{Bn^{m_{\nu}}}\right]\right)\longrightarrow 0.`$ |  |

By
Lemmas [23](#Thmlemma23 "Lemma 23 (Exact transfer between task inputs). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and [24](#Thmlemma24 "Lemma 24 (From successor suppression to total old-path mass). ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
this event also gives
Equation [112](#A2.E112 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
Since $`\nu\in(0,1/2)`$,
Equation [94](#A2.E94 "In B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
implies

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{map}}\theta M_{\mathrm{old}}\bar{\epsilon}_{n}(\delta^{\prime})^{\lambda}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\widetilde{O}\!\left(C_{\mathrm{map}}\theta M_{\mathrm{old}}C_{\mathrm{new}}^{\lambda}|\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{new}}|^{\lambda}\left[\frac{1+B^{-1}\log(1/\delta^{\prime})}{n^{\nu}}\right]^{\lambda}\right).`$ |  | (114) |

For the late-phase statement, take $`\delta^{\prime}=\delta`$ and
combine
Equations [112](#A2.E112 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
[100](#A2.E100 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
and
[102](#A2.E102 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

If $`\theta=0`$,
Equations [96](#A2.E96 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and
[98](#A2.E98 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proof of Theorem . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
give

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}\!\left(Y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\mathrm{old}}\mid Y_{0}=y_{\mathrm{old},0},x_{\mathrm{old}}\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle=p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}.`$ |  | (115) |

∎

See
[1](#Thmremark1 "Remark 1 (Explicit representation of ℭ_old⁢(ϕ)). ‣ Forgetting in SFT ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")

#### Sketch of proof of Remark [1](#Thmremark1 "Remark 1 (Explicit representation of ℭ_old⁢(ϕ)). ‣ Forgetting in SFT ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

- •
  Bound the mass of the largest admissible subset using the largest
  individual old-path mass.
- •
  Obtain a second bound by subtracting the smallest omitted masses from
  the total, and combine the two bounds.

#### Main proof of Remark [1](#Thmremark1 "Remark 1 (Explicit representation of ℭ_old⁢(ϕ)). ‣ Forgetting in SFT ‣ 3.2 Continual learning and catastrophic forgetting ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof.

Let

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle p_{(1)}^{\mathrm{old}}\geq\cdots\geq p_{(M_{\mathrm{old}})}^{\mathrm{old}}`$ |  |

be the ordered values of
$`\{P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma):\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}\}`$.
For $`\phi\in[0,1]`$, put $`q=\lfloor\phi M_{\mathrm{old}}\rfloor`$.
Then

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(\phi)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{q}p_{(i)}^{\mathrm{old}}\leq q\max_{\gamma\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{old}}}P_{\mathrm{pre}}^{\mathrm{old}}(\gamma)\leq p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}\kappa_{\max}^{\mathrm{old}}\phi.`$ |  | (116) |

Moreover, $`M_{\mathrm{old}}-q\geq(1-\phi)M_{\mathrm{old}}`$, so

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(\phi)`$ | $`\displaystyle=p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}-\sum_{i=q+1}^{M_{\mathrm{old}}}p_{(i)}^{\mathrm{old}}\leq p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}\left[1-\kappa_{\min}^{\mathrm{old}}(1-\phi)\right].`$ |  | (117) |

Combining
Equations [116](#A2.E116 "In Proof. ‣ Main proof of Remark . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and
[117](#A2.E117 "In Proof. ‣ Main proof of Remark . ‣ B.7 Catastrophic forgetting under SFT ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathfrak{C}_{\mathrm{old}}(\phi)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq p_{\mathrm{old}}^{\mathrm{pre}}\min\left\{\kappa_{\max}^{\mathrm{old}}\phi,1-\kappa_{\min}^{\mathrm{old}}+\kappa_{\min}^{\mathrm{old}}\phi\right\}.`$ |  | (118) |

∎

### B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability

###### Proposition 3 (Uniform restartability).

Suppose
Assumptions [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (transition uniqueness and competition between tasks). ‣ Continual learning. ‣ 2.1 Continual learning on CoT graph ‣ 2 Problem Setup ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and [2](#Thmassumption2 "Assumption 2 (Pre-trained diversity). ‣ Pre-training requirements ‣ 2.2 Pre-training and fine-tuning ‣ 2 Problem Setup ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
hold, $`\eta_{j}=\Theta(j^{-\nu})`$ for fixed $`0\leq\nu<1/2`$, and
$`0<\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\delta,\epsilon_{\mathrm{rel}}<1`$. If
$`n`$ satisfies
Equation [20](#S3.E20 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
with a sufficiently large uniform constant, then with probability at
least $`1-\delta`$ over training,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\inf_{h:\mathcal{S}_{\ell,h}\neq\varnothing}\ \inf_{s\in\mathcal{S}_{\ell,h}}\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}(y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\ell}\mid y_{0}=s,x_{\ell})\geq 1-O(KM\epsilon_{1}),
``` |  | (119) |

where $`\mathcal{S}_{\ell,h}`$ is the set of relevant starts in
$`C_{h}`$.

See
[1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")

#### Sketch of proofs of Propositions [3](#Thmproposition3 "Proposition 3 (Uniform restartability). ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning") and [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

- •
  Show that the stated training length implies the effective-drift bound
  required for transition learning
  (Lemma [25](#Thmlemma25 "Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Under full coverage, learn all correct transitions on a common event
  and convert their accuracy into successful reasoning from every
  relevant start
  (Lemmas [10](#Thmlemma10 "Lemma 10 (Simultaneous learning with finite mini-batches). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
  and [11](#Thmlemma11 "Lemma 11 (Converting edge accuracy to rollout accuracy). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Under the rare-entrance condition, bound the increase in visitation
  probability throughout training
  (Lemma [26](#Thmlemma26 "Lemma 26 (Control of the increase in visitation). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Use this visitation bound to establish nonvisitation across all
  adaptively generated training rollouts
  (Lemma [27](#Thmlemma27 "Lemma 27 (Nonvisitation throughout adaptive training). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Learn the transitions reachable without the rare entrance and bound
  the final error from the original start
  (Lemmas [9](#Thmlemma9 "Lemma 9 (Update opportunities from retained coverage). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"), [10](#Thmlemma10 "Lemma 10 (Simultaneous learning with finite mini-batches). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
  and [11](#Thmlemma11 "Lemma 11 (Converting edge accuracy to rollout accuracy). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Show that nonvisitation leaves the downstream cluster unchanged and
  bounds its restart accuracy
  (Lemma [28](#Thmlemma28 "Lemma 28 (An unvisited downstream cluster remains unlearned). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).
- •
  Intersect the learning and nonvisitation events to obtain the joint
  original-start and restart guarantees
  (Proposition [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).

###### Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition).

Under the learning-rate conditions of
Proposition [3](#Thmproposition3 "Proposition 3 (Uniform restartability). ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
Equation [20](#S3.E20 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
with a sufficiently large uniform constant implies

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathfrak{D}_{\ell,n}^{(K)}(\delta/2)\geq\frac{\epsilon_{1}^{-1}-1}{c_{\mathrm{int}}^{2}\epsilon_{2}^{2}\log(1/\epsilon_{\mathrm{rel}})}.
``` |  | (120) |

###### Proof.

For fixed $`0\leq\nu<1/2`$ and uniform learning-rate constants,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{j=1}^{n}\eta_{j}=\Theta(n^{1-\nu}),\qquad\sum_{j=1}^{n}\eta_{j}^{2}=\Theta(n^{1-2\nu}).
``` |  |

The ratio of the square-root term in
Equation [47](#A2.E47 "In B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
to its positive drift term is

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
O\!\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(2KM/\delta)}{B\underline{\rho}_{\ell,K,M}^{\,2}n}}\right).
``` |  |

The first term of the maximum in
Equation [20](#S3.E20 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
with a sufficiently large constant, makes this ratio at most one half.
Consequently the effective drift is
$`\Omega(\underline{\rho}_{\ell,K,M}n^{1-\nu})`$. The second term in
that maximum, again with a sufficiently large constant, makes this lower
bound at least the right-hand side of
Equation [120](#A2.E120 "In Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
This proves the implication. ∎

###### Lemma 26 (Control of the increase in visitation).

Let $`N=N_{\ell}=|E_{\ell}|`$, the total number of correct inter-cluster
edges of the task, be fixed. Under the retained initialization
condition, for every training history and iteration $`j`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(\exists t<T:y_{t}=u_{m}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\leq\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(0)}}(\exists t<T:y_{t}=u_{m}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\frac{c_{\mathrm{int}}^{-N}}{\epsilon_{2}^{N}}.`$ |  | (121) |

###### Proof.

Iterating the second inequality of
Equation [51](#A2.E51 "In Lemma 7 (Local update and invariant interior transitions). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
from $`1`$ to $`j`$ gives

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\frac{p_{u_{i},v}^{(j)}}{p_{u_{i},v}^{(0)}}\leq\frac{p_{i}^{(j)}}{p_{i}^{(0)}}\leq\frac{1}{p_{i}^{(0)}}\qquad((u_{i},v)\in E).
``` |  |

This applies to every outgoing edge at an updated source, including
intra-cluster edges. At other sources the ratio is one. Factor the
probability of a complete rollout into its one-step Markov
probabilities, using the same stopping rule for both predictors. Each
updated source occurs at most once by node simplicity. Sources that do
not occur can be included because $`p_{i}^{(j)}/p_{i}^{(0)}\geq 1`$.
Consequently,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j)}}(y_{0:T}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\leq\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(0)}}(y_{0:T}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\prod_{i=1}^{N_{\ell}}\frac{p_{i}^{(j)}}{p_{i}^{(0)}}.
``` |  | (122) |

The retained initialization condition gives
$`p_{i}^{(0)}\geq c_{\mathrm{int}}\epsilon_{2}`$, and hence

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\prod_{i=1}^{N_{\ell}}\frac{1}{p_{i}^{(0)}}\leq c_{\mathrm{int}}^{-N}\epsilon_{2}^{-N}.
``` |  | (123) |

Sum
Equation [122](#A2.E122 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
over the node-simple rollouts that visit $`u_{m}`$ before stopping, and
use $`p_{i}^{(j)}\leq 1`$ together with
Equation [123](#A2.E123 "In Proof. ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
∎

###### Lemma 27 (Nonvisitation throughout adaptive training).

Under
Assumption [3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Existence of a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\!\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\mathbf{1}\{\tau_{m}^{(b,j)}<T^{(b,j)}\}>0\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\leq Bn\,\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(0)}}(\exists t<T:y_{t}=u_{m}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\frac{c_{\mathrm{int}}^{-N}}{\epsilon_{2}^{N}}=O\!\left(\frac{Bn}{M^{\prime}\epsilon_{2}^{N}}\right).`$ |  | (124) |

###### Proof.

Conditional on $`\mathcal{F}_{j-1}`$, the student rollout has law
$`\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j-1)}}`$.
Lemma [26](#Thmlemma26 "Lemma 26 (Control of the increase in visitation). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
therefore gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}\{\tau_{m}^{(b,j)}<T^{(b,j)}\}\mid\mathcal{F}_{j-1}]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad=\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(j-1)}}(\exists t<T:y_{t}=u_{m}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\leq\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(0)}}(\exists t<T:y_{t}=u_{m}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\frac{c_{\mathrm{int}}^{-N}}{\epsilon_{2}^{N}}.`$ |  |

Here $`T^{(b,j)}`$ is the random length of that sampled rollout. Take
total expectations and sum over $`b,j`$. The probability of a positive
visit count is at most its expectation, proving the claim. ∎

###### Lemma 28 (An unvisited downstream cluster remains unlearned).

Under
Assumptions [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (transition uniqueness and competition between tasks). ‣ Continual learning. ‣ 2.1 Continual learning on CoT graph ‣ 2 Problem Setup ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and [3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Existence of a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
if no training rollout visits $`u_{m}`$, then
$`\mathbf{W}^{(n)}e_{u,v}=\mathbf{W}^{(0)}e_{u,v}`$ for every
$`u\in C_{k}`$ and $`(u,v)\in E`$, and
Equation [23](#S3.E23 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
holds.

###### Proof.

Every training rollout starts outside $`C_{k}`$. Its only entrance is
$`e_{m}`$, so nonvisitation of $`u_{m}`$ implies nonvisitation of all of
$`C_{k}`$.
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 (Local update and invariant interior transitions). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
iterated over the training steps, therefore leaves all columns with
source in $`C_{k}`$ unchanged, even if a hint contains an edge inside
$`C_{k}`$.

Success from $`s\in\mathcal{S}_{\ell,k}`$ requires leaving
$`C_{k}\neq\tilde{C}_{\ell}`$. Since $`s`$ is a relevant start, a
correct path from $`y_{\ell,0}`$ has a prefix ending at $`s`$.
Concatenating this prefix with any successful continuation from $`s`$
gives a correct path: a repeated vertex would create a directed cycle.
Consequently, the first exit of a successful continuation belongs to
$`E_{\ell}`$. By
Assumption [1](#Thmassumption1 "Assumption 1 (transition uniqueness and competition between tasks). ‣ Continual learning. ‣ 2.1 Continual learning on CoT graph ‣ 2 Problem Setup ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
at most one such exit leaves each $`u\in\partial_{\ell}^{\star}C_{k}`$,
so there are at most $`|\partial_{\ell}^{\star}C_{k}|\leq M`$ such
edges. For each $`(u,v)\in E_{\ell}`$ with $`u\in C_{k}`$, the Markov
property gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}\!\left[\sum_{t<T}\mathbf{1}\{y_{t}=u,y_{t+1}=v\}\mid y_{0}=s,x_{\ell}\right]`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad=\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}(v\mid u,x_{\ell})\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}\!\left[\sum_{t<T}\mathbf{1}\{y_{t}=u\}\mid y_{0}=s,x_{\ell}\right]\leq\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(0)}}(v\mid u,x_{\ell}).`$ |  |

The last step uses parameter preservation and node simplicity. A union
bound over these correct exit edges and the retained initialization
condition yield

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}(y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\ell}\mid y_{0}=s,x_{\ell})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(u,v)\in E_{\ell}\\
u\in C_{k}\end{subarray}}\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(0)}}(v\mid u,x_{\ell})=O\!\left(|\partial_{\ell}^{\star}C_{k}|\epsilon_{2}\right)=O(M\epsilon_{2}).`$ |  |

The bound is uniform over $`s\in\mathcal{S}_{\ell,k}`$, proving
Equation [23](#S3.E23 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
∎

#### Main proofs of Propositions [3](#Thmproposition3 "Proposition 3 (Uniform restartability). ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning") and [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof of Proposition [3](#Thmproposition3 "Proposition 3 (Uniform restartability). ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

Lemma [25](#Thmlemma25 "Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
first gives
Equation [120](#A2.E120 "In Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
from the stated condition on $`n`$. By
Lemma [10](#Thmlemma10 "Lemma 10 (Simultaneous learning with finite mini-batches). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
with failure budget $`\delta/2`$ and
Equation [120](#A2.E120 "In Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
all $`N_{\ell}`$ correct edges have error at most $`\epsilon_{1}`$ with
probability at least $`1-\delta/2`$. Fix any such training history.
Lemma [11](#Thmlemma11 "Lemma 11 (Converting edge accuracy to rollout accuracy). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives, for every relevant start,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
1-\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}(y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\ell}\mid y_{0}=s,x_{\ell})\leq[C_{\mathrm{bad}}(1+N_{\ell})+N_{\ell}]\epsilon_{1}\leq(1+2C_{\mathrm{bad}})KM\epsilon_{1}.
``` |  |

The same history works for every $`s`$, so no additional probabilistic
union bound is needed. This is stronger than the stated confidence
$`1-\delta`$. ∎

###### Proof of Proposition [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

The common condition on $`n`$ implies
Equation [120](#A2.E120 "In Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
by
Lemma [25](#Thmlemma25 "Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
Hold $`N=N_{\ell}=|E_{\ell}|`$ fixed. Let $`C/M^{\prime}`$ be the
initial visitation bound in
Assumption [3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Existence of a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
and take the uniform constant in
Equation [21](#S3.E21 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
to be at least $`2Cc_{\mathrm{int}}^{-N}`$. Then
Equation [21](#S3.E21 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and initial rarity imply

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(0)}}(\exists t<T:y_{t}=u_{m}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\frac{c_{\mathrm{int}}^{-N}}{\epsilon_{2}^{N}}\leq\min\!\left\{KM\epsilon_{1},\frac{\delta}{2Bn}\right\}.`$ |  | (125) |

Lemma [27](#Thmlemma27 "Lemma 27 (Nonvisitation throughout adaptive training). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
makes the probability that any training rollout visits $`u_{m}`$ at most
$`\delta/2`$. Independently of whether nonvisitation holds,
Lemma [10](#Thmlemma10 "Lemma 10 (Simultaneous learning with finite mini-batches). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
with failure budget $`\delta/2`$ and
Equation [120](#A2.E120 "In Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
give error at most $`\epsilon_{1}`$ for all edges specified in
Lemma [9](#Thmlemma9 "Lemma 9 (Update opportunities from retained coverage). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
except on a set of probability at most $`\delta/2`$. A union bound gives
both conclusions jointly with probability at least $`1-\delta`$; the two
events need not be independent.

Fix a history in their intersection.
Lemma [26](#Thmlemma26 "Lemma 26 (Control of the increase in visitation). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and
Equation [125](#A2.E125 "In Proof of Proposition . ‣ Main proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
bound the final evaluation visitation probability by $`KM\epsilon_{1}`$.
In
Equation [57](#A2.E57 "In Lemma 11 (Converting edge accuracy to rollout accuracy). ‣ Shared notation and constants. ‣ B.3 Learning hint-supported inter-cluster transitions ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
this yields

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
1-\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}(y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\ell}\mid y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell})\leq[C_{\mathrm{bad}}(1+KM)+2KM]\epsilon_{1}\leq(2+2C_{\mathrm{bad}})KM\epsilon_{1}.
``` |  |

Lemma [28](#Thmlemma28 "Lemma 28 (An unvisited downstream cluster remains unlearned). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives the restart upper bound on that same history. This proves all
three assertions, including the nonvisit event, within confidence
$`1-\delta`$. ∎

See
[3](#Thmremark3 "Remark 3 (Separation of training times). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")

#### Sketch of proof of Remark [3](#Thmremark3 "Remark 3 (Separation of training times). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

Express the rare-entrance budget as a nonvisitation horizon and compare
it with the sufficient training length for uniform restartability
(Propositions [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and [3](#Thmproposition3 "Proposition 3 (Uniform restartability). ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).

#### Main proof of Remark [3](#Thmremark3 "Remark 3 (Separation of training times). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof.

With $`N=N_{\ell}=|E_{\ell}|`$ fixed and the constant choice in the
proof of
Proposition [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
rearranging
Equation [21](#S3.E21 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
M^{\prime}=\Omega\!\left(\frac{1}{KM\epsilon_{1}}\epsilon_{2}^{-N}\right),\qquad n=O\!\left(\frac{\delta M^{\prime}}{B}\epsilon_{2}^{N}\right).
``` |  | (126) |

Thus the allowed nonvisit horizon grows linearly in $`M^{\prime}`$ with
other quantities fixed. In addition to the first condition, if that
horizon is $`\omega`$ of the sufficient learning scale in
Equation [20](#S3.E20 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
their ratio diverges. The comparison uses common bounds on the other
problem parameters and confidence $`1-\delta`$ in each setting at each
fixed chosen horizon. ∎

###### Remark 4 (The scale of initial rarity).

The sufficient conditions of
Proposition [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
imply $`M^{\prime}=\Omega(M^{2}\log M)`$, so $`M^{\prime}`$ grows
asymptotically faster than $`M`$.

#### Sketch of proof of Remark [4](#Thmremark4 "Remark 4 (The scale of initial rarity). ‣ Main proof of Remark . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

Derive the minimum batch budget imposed by the learning condition and
substitute it into the rarity requirement to obtain the coarse rarity
scale
(Lemma [29](#Thmlemma29 "Lemma 29 (The coarse rarity scale). ‣ Sketch of proof of Remark . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")).

###### Lemma 29 (The coarse rarity scale).

The sufficient conditions
Equation [20](#S3.E20 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and
Equation [21](#S3.E21 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
imply $`M^{\prime}=\Omega(M^{2}\log M)`$ as $`M\to\infty`$.

###### Proof.

Since $`\epsilon_{1}<1`$,
Equation [120](#A2.E120 "In Lemma 25 (The stated training length implies the drift condition). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
forces the drift to be positive. Squaring its defining strict inequality
gives

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\underline{\rho}_{\ell,K,M}^{\,2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\eta_{j}\right)^{2}>\frac{\log(2KM/\delta)}{2B}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\eta_{j}^{2}.
``` |  |

Cauchy–Schwarz, $`(\sum_{j}\eta_{j})^{2}\leq n\sum_{j}\eta_{j}^{2}`$,
gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Bn>\frac{\log(2KM/\delta)}{2\underline{\rho}_{\ell,K,M}^{\,2}}.
``` |  | (127) |

Substitution in
Equation [21](#S3.E21 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
M^{\prime}=\Omega\!\left(\left[\epsilon_{2}^{-N}\right]\frac{\log(2KM/\delta)}{\delta\underline{\rho}_{\ell,K,M}^{\,2}}\right).
``` |  | (128) |

Since $`\underline{\rho}_{\ell,K,M}\leq c_{\mathrm{route}}/M`$ and
$`\epsilon_{2}^{-N}\geq 1`$, for $`M\to\infty`$ this implies

|     |                                                              |     |       |
|-----|--------------------------------------------------------------|-----|-------|
|     |
       ``` math
       M^{\prime}=\Omega(M^{2}\log M),\qquad M^{\prime}/M\to\infty.
       ```                                                           |     | (129) |

The fuller budget
Equation [21](#S3.E21 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
remains required for
Proposition [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
∎

#### Main proof of Remark [4](#Thmremark4 "Remark 4 (The scale of initial rarity). ‣ Main proof of Remark . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").

###### Proof.

Lemma [29](#Thmlemma29 "Lemma 29 (The coarse rarity scale). ‣ Sketch of proof of Remark . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
gives the claimed scale $`M^{\prime}=\Omega(M^{2}\log M)`$. This coarse
implication does not replace the full budget
Equation [21](#S3.E21 "In Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
required for
Proposition [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning").
∎

###### Remark 5 (Correct paths bypassing the rare entrance).

On the event in the proof of
Proposition [1](#Thmproposition1 "Proposition 1 (Restart failure beyond a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
Equations [121](#A2.E121 "In Lemma 26 (Control of the increase in visitation). ‣ Sketch of proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
and [125](#A2.E125 "In Proof of Proposition . ‣ Main proofs of Propositions and . ‣ B.8 Pre-training and intermediate restartability ‣ Appendix B Realizability and proofs ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning")
bound the final visitation probability of $`u^{*}=u_{m}`$ by
$`KM\epsilon_{1}`$. Subtracting this probability from the original-start
accuracy bound proved above gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\pi_{\mathbf{W}^{(n)}}\!\left(y_{T}\in\tilde{C}_{\ell},\ y_{t}\neq u^{*}\ \forall\,0\leq t<T\,\middle|\,y_{\ell,0},x_{\ell}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\geq 1-(3+2C_{\mathrm{bad}})KM\epsilon_{1}.`$ |  |

For $`(3+2C_{\mathrm{bad}})KM\epsilon_{1}<1`$, this probability is
positive, so a correct path avoiding $`u^{*}`$ exists. By the
unique-entrance condition in
Assumption [3](#Thmassumption3 "Assumption 3 (Existence of a rare entrance). ‣ 3.3 A theoretical implication for pre-training diversity ‣ 3 Main results ‣ Learning without Overwriting: A Theory of Self-Distillation and Supervised Fine-Tuning in Continual Reasoning"),
such a path also bypasses $`C_{k}`$.

## Appendix C Numerical experiment

Code, configuration files, saved results, and reproduction instructions
are provided in the [GitHub
````
